# 2015 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** W1 shipped 2026-09-05 — dest-name hops on gold + official landings · Facebook leftover CTA is Like a leftover page (not Campus). Keys / official 10 / guided 6 / star unchanged. Look before the next year.
**Feel:** Go LIVE
**Star:** Periscope Go LIVE · `itt15-periscope` · `sites/periscope/index.html`
**ILS June:** 863,105,652 (−11%) · 3,185,996,155*
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 267
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt15-periscope`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2015/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2015/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2015 — dual scale · bans
2. Periscope — title then Go LIVE
3. Google Photos — backup HQ
4. Windows 10 — free upgrade
5. Apple Music — 3-month trial
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt15-periscope`

**Room:** `years/2015/sites/periscope/index.html`
**Hook:** `data-peri-live`
**Nostalgia:** Periscope Go LIVE — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Empty title · Stories-as-2015 gold trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt15-periscope`.

**Complete writes**
- Title ≥2 · Go LIVE. Writes itt15-periscope.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2015` · key `itt15-periscope`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-periscope` — do not write `itt14-*` or `itt16-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Google Photos → `years/2015/sites/googlephotos/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 267 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt15-adblock-ab` · sites/adblock/about.html

**Open:** `/years/2015/sites/adblock/about.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="adblock-ab"])`
**Need:** needPick=`keep` · field ≥2 (placeholder “2015 leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `google` · `youtube`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="adblock-ab"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt15-adblock-ab']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick (not `keep`) + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt15-periscope` stays empty. `itt14-adblock-ab` and `itt16-adblock-ab` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click `[data-lo-pick="keep"]`. Wrong pick never writes.
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “2015 leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="adblock-ab"]`.
- `localStorage['itt15-adblock-ab']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2015", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt15-adblock-ab`.
- Does **not** write `itt15-periscope`.
- **Next:** ★ Periscope → `/years/2015/sites/periscope/index.html`
